# The Fade / Continue Book

Spec: forge/FADE_CONTINUE_BOOK_SPEC.md. Every pass of every Vol Forecast v3 line, 2016 → 2026-08. Pooled: 526,570 passes over 16 instruments in σ units; EURUSD alone: 31,024; six indices pooled: 149,428. Continue = reached the next line out first; fade = reached the line behind first; stall = neither by the London day end. Break-even = the continue rate a follow trade needs at that line spacing (before spread). R = net of spread.

**Edge rows:** 9 of 1446 rows (2892 tests, Benjamini–Hochberg 10%: 9 survive before the both-halves check).

## Note on the index and gold edge rows (checked 2026-10-01)
The rows flagged on NQ, SPX and gold are follow trades whose profit is long-side drift: split by side, NQ follows on UP
lines (longs) make +0.023R (+0.003 in 2016–22, +0.061 in 2023–26) and on DOWN lines (shorts) −0.026R; SPX +0.031 / −0.041;
DE30 +0.027 / −0.017. A line edge would work both ways. The remaining flagged rows (gold Asia, US2000, DOW) sit on 30–49
touches. Read the index views as "the 2023–26 bull market rewarded longs", not as a line edge.
